# 16.Testing — State Map

> **What this file is:** Phase and task tracker for all work within `16.Testing`.
> **What it is not:** The root tracker — that lives at `state-map.md`.
> **Sync policy:** When all tasks under a Phase Key are `●`, run `/state-map-phase` with `phase_key: SK.16.{Phase}` to propagate that milestone to the root state-map.

---

## Legend

| Symbol | Meaning |
|--------|---------|
| `○` | Not started |
| `◐` | In progress |
| `●` | Complete |
| `⚑` | Blocked |
| `—` | N/A / Skipped |

---

## Phase Key Registry

> Phase keys are the sync bridge between this sub-state-map and the root `state-map.md`.
> Each key maps a local milestone to a root-level phase. When a key's Promotion Condition is met, the root is updated via `/state-map-phase`.

| Phase Key | Maps to Root Phase | Promotion Condition |
|-----------|---------------------|----------------------|
| `SK.16.Design` | Design | All tasks in Phase: Design are `●` |
| `SK.16.Scaffold` | Scaffold | All tasks in Phase: Scaffold are `●` |
| `SK.16.Core` | Core | All tasks in Phase: Core are `●` |
| `SK.16.Tests` | Tests | All tasks in Phase: Tests are `●` |
| `SK.16.Docs` | Docs | All tasks in Phase: Docs are `●` |
| `SK.16.Published` | Published | All tasks in Phase: Published are `●` |

P-035, P-064, P-179–P-187 populate the phase tables below (this pass). See per-task `Source` notes inline.

P-226/WO-036 (audit-first phase, depends on `05.Application`'s P-220/P-224 — design-only as of 2026-06-30) adds D-55–D-58, S-15, C-47, T-38, DO-13.

P-244/P-245 (WO-040) add D-59–D-71, S-16–S-18, C-48–C-53, T-39–T-44, DO-14/DO-15.

P-258 (WO-041, depends on P-249/01.Core and P-250/00.Governance for the wider logging-standard context only — not a hard build dependency) adds D-72–D-78, S-19/S-20, C-54–C-59, T-45, DO-16.

P-268/P-269 (WO-043) add D-79–D-96, S-21–S-24, C-60–C-63, T-46/T-47, DO-17/DO-18. P-268 (`MinioContainerFixture`) is fully unblocked. P-269 (`InMemoryFileStorage`/`InMemoryBlobUriGenerator`) was originally a genuine compile-time blocker, not a soft design-ahead situation: `08.Storage.Abstractions` shipped zero compiled types at the original design pass (verified on disk), so C-61–C-63/T-47/DO-18 were marked `⚑` Blocked. **CORRECTED 2026-07-17**: re-verified on disk during the Design-phase confirmation pass — `08.Storage`'s `SK.08.Core` is now `●` 30/30 with real `IFileStorage`/`IBlobUriGenerator`/model/`StorageErrors` code shipped, zero drift from the target shape below. The blocker has cleared; C-61–C-63/T-47/DO-18 are corrected back to `○` Pending (state-correction only — implementation remains future Core/Tests/Docs-phase work). See the Blocked section and Cross-Domain Dependencies table below, both updated to reflect this.

`16.Testing` is exempt from the platform's normal downward-only layering direction (root `CLAUDE.md`: "`16.Testing` may reference any layer — test infrastructure only, never shipped"). This table tracks which abstraction packages each fake needs, not a layering constraint. `13.ServiceDefaults`'s `SharedKernel.MultiTenancy` (`ITenantResolutionStrategy`) is deliberately **excluded** — P-187's `FakeTenantResolutionStrategy` is structurally compatible only, no project reference taken.

| D-55 | **Audit** `AmbientActivityTestHelper` (`Communication/`) against P-220's `TracingBehavior` test need (span-per-request assertion with `request.name` tag, per `05.Application/CLAUDE.md`'s own Test Rules note citing `07.Messaging`'s `OTelInstrumentationTests` pattern). Finding: `AmbientActivityTestHelper` only *sets* `Activity.Current` to a caller-supplied `Activity` for propagation/correlation-id tests and registers a static always-sampling `ActivityListener` scoped to its own private `ActivitySource` — it has no capability to *record/capture* `Activity` instances started against a different, externally-owned `ActivitySource` (`ApplicationDiagnostics.ActivitySource`, name `"SharedKernel.Application"`, version `"1.0.0"`) and assert on their tags/duration/count after the fact. This is a distinct capability (a recording listener) from what exists today (an ambient-context setter). Determination: **not sufficient as-is — a minimal additive helper is needed**, not a duplicate of `AmbientActivityTestHelper`, not a replacement for it. Source: P-226/WO-036. RE-VERIFIED (2026-06-30): read `Communication/AmbientActivityTestHelper.cs` directly — confirms it owns a private `ActivitySource("SharedKernel.Testing.Communication")` and only ever calls `Source.StartActivity(...)` on itself, setting `Activity.Current`; zero capability to listen to/record an externally-owned `ActivitySource`. Read `05.Application/CLAUDE.md` plus the live `ApplicationDiagnostics.cs`/`TracingBehavior.cs` source — confirms `ActivitySource = new("SharedKernel.Application", "1.0.0")` and `StartActivity("Request.Handle")` tagging `request.name`, exactly as documented here. Audit finding stands unchanged. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-56 | Design `ActivityRecorder` (sealed class, implements `IDisposable`) — new, additive type in `Communication/` (`SharedKernel.Testing.Communication` namespace; same folder as `AmbientActivityTestHelper`, since both are ambient-`Activity`/OTel test-support helpers, not a new capability folder): `static .StartRecording(string activitySourceName)` → `ActivityRecorder` (registers a process-scoped `ActivityListener` filtered to the named `ActivitySource` only — e.g. `"SharedKernel.Application"` — with `Sample = AllDataAndRecorded`); `.RecordedActivities` → `IReadOnlyList<Activity>` (every `Activity` started against that source while recording is active, in start order); `.Dispose()` → unregisters the listener (`ActivityListener` is itself `IDisposable`) so no test leaks a listener registration into the next one. Deliberately scoped to one named `ActivitySource` per instance (not global) — multiple tests recording different sources concurrently never interfere with each other, unlike the single static listener `AmbientActivityTestHelper` owns for its own private source. Zero dependency beyond `System.Diagnostics` (BCL) — no new `PackageReference`. Source: P-226/WO-036. RE-VERIFIED (2026-06-30): design confirmed sound — `ActivityRecorder.cs` correctly does not yet exist on disk (deferred to C-47/Core phase, still `○`); shape requires no new project/package reference, matching `AmbientActivityTestHelper`'s existing zero-extra-dependency footprint. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-57 | **Audit** `FakeCacheService`/`FakeCacheInvalidationBus` (`Caching/`) against P-224's `CacheInvalidationBehavior` test need (assert `ICacheService.RemoveAsync`/`RemoveByTagAsync` called once per declared key/tag on success, never on failure/exception). Finding: `FakeCacheService` already implements `ICacheService` in full, including `.RemoveAsync(string key, CancellationToken ct)` and `.RemoveByTagAsync(string tag, CancellationToken ct)` (see existing `Caching/` Interface Contracts block) — both are real, callable members backed by the fake's thread-safe `ConcurrentDictionary` state, and a test can assert post-call cache-population state (key absent, tag-evicted) directly through `FakeCacheService`'s existing surface (no new query/assertion method needed; absence-after-removal is directly observable). `FakeCacheInvalidationBus` is a different, unrelated abstraction (`ICacheInvalidationBus` — Redis pub/sub cross-service cache-invalidation *signaling*, owned by `02.Caching.Redis.PubSub`) and has no bearing on `CacheInvalidationBehavior`, which calls `ICacheService` directly, never `ICacheInvalidationBus`. Determination: **`FakeCacheService` already covers this test need in full — no new fake required.** This phase's cache-invalidation scope reduces to documentation only (recording the determination), per the phase's own acceptance criteria. Source: P-226/WO-036. | D-72 | Design `LogRecord` (sealed record) — one captured log entry: `.EventId` (`Microsoft.Extensions.Logging.EventId`), `.LogLevel`, `.Message` (fully formatted string, via the caller-supplied `Func<TState,Exception?,string>` formatter — never re-derived), `.State` (`IReadOnlyList<KeyValuePair<string,object?>>?` — populated only when `TState` implements that interface, which is exactly the shape both `[LoggerMessage]`'s source-generated state struct and standard structured-logging calls produce; `null` otherwise), `.Exception` (`Exception?`), `.Scopes` (`IReadOnlyList<object?>`, outer-to-inner order — a snapshot of the active `BeginScope` stack at the moment this record was logged). Add `.TryGetProperty(string name, out object? value)` — scans `.State` for a `KeyValuePair` whose `Key` exactly matches `name` (case-sensitive, matching the root `CLAUDE.md` logging convention's PascalCase named-placeholder rule) — the primitive `LoggerAssertions.ShouldHaveLoggedWithProperty` is built on. | D-73 | Design `InMemoryLogger` (sealed class, implements `Microsoft.Extensions.Logging.ILogger`) — `.Log<TState>(LogLevel, EventId, TState, Exception?, Func<TState,Exception?,string>)` appends a `LogRecord` (per D-72) to a thread-safe `ConcurrentQueue<LogRecord>`; `.IsEnabled(LogLevel)` compares against a settable `.MinLevel` property (default `LogLevel.Trace` — captures everything by default, mirroring `FakeUserContext`'s "zero-configuration by default" convention); `.BeginScope<TState>(TState state)` pushes `state` onto an `AsyncLocal<ScopeNode?>`-backed immutable linked-list scope stack and returns an `IDisposable` that pops exactly that node on `Dispose()` — `AsyncLocal`, not a plain field/thread-static, so nested `using (logger.BeginScope(...))` blocks compose correctly across `await` boundaries the same way a real logging provider's scope stack does, and so parallel xUnit test collections never cross-contaminate each other's scope state (this package's standing thread-safety rule). `.Records` → `IReadOnlyList<LogRecord>` (snapshot of the queue). `.Clear()` → `void` (empties the queue — for multi-phase single-test assertions). Source: P-258/WO-041. CONFIRMED (this pass): verbatim match against `CLAUDE.md` lines 780–794. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-74 | Design `InMemoryLoggerFactory` (sealed class, implements `Microsoft.Extensions.Logging.ILoggerFactory`) — `.CreateLogger(string categoryName)` returns/creates a per-category `InMemoryLogger` via `ConcurrentDictionary<string,InMemoryLogger>.GetOrAdd` (auto-creates — tests never need to pre-register a category); `.AddProvider(ILoggerProvider)` — documented no-op (this fake IS the entire logging pipeline for the test; it does not compose with additional providers); `.Dispose()` — no-op (nothing to release); `.GetLogger(string categoryName)` → `InMemoryLogger` (same `GetOrAdd` semantics as `CreateLogger`, exposed under a more discoverable name for assertion call sites); `.Loggers` → `IReadOnlyDictionary<string,InMemoryLogger>` (snapshot of every category created so far). | D-85 | Design `InMemoryFileStorage` (sealed, implements `IFileStorage` from `08.Storage.Abstractions`, verified against the live 9-member contract — CORRECTED at this Design-confirmation pass (2026-07-17): the interface has nine members, not ten as previously and repeatedly miscounted in this file and `CLAUDE.md`; re-read directly from `08.Storage/SharedKernel.Storage.Abstractions/Abstractions/IFileStorage.cs`, which now exists as real compiled code): backing store as `ConcurrentDictionary<(string Bucket, string Key), StoredObject>` where `StoredObject` holds fully-buffered content bytes (read once from the caller's `Stream` during `UploadAsync` — the fake never disposes the caller-owned `Content` stream, per `FileUploadRequest`'s documented ownership contract), `ContentType`, `Metadata`, a deterministic incrementing ETag (`Interlocked`-based sequence, never a random `Guid`), null `VersionId`, and a fixed non-real `LastModified` instant (never `DateTimeOffset.UtcNow`, mirroring `FakeClock`'s own "fixed instant" philosophy — implemented as an internal fixed baseline, NOT a dependency on `Clocks/FakeClock`, since sibling capability folders must never reference each other). NOTE: `FileDeleteOutcome` (verified live) is constructed via its static `Success(key)`/`Failure(key, error)` factory methods, not an object initializer — a future Core-phase implementer must call these factories, not attempt `new FileDeleteOutcome { ... }`. Source: P-269/WO-043. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-86 | Design `UploadAsync`/`DownloadAsync`/`DeleteAsync`/`ExistsAsync`/`GetMetadataAsync` member behavior — each mirrors the documented contract exactly: `DownloadAsync` returns `Result.Failure(StorageErrors.NotFound)` for a missing key, wraps stored bytes in a fresh `MemoryStream` per call (each `FileDownload` independently disposable); `ExistsAsync` returns `Result.Success(false)` for missing keys with no side effect; `DeleteAsync` is idempotent — deleting an absent key still returns `Result.Success`; `GetMetadataAsync` returns `NotFound` for a missing key. CONFIRMED (2026-07-17): behavior verified consistent with the live `IFileStorage.cs`/`StorageErrors.cs` on disk. Source: P-269/WO-043. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-87 | Design `CopyAsync` — a server-side "copy" is a dictionary-to-dictionary duplication of the stored bytes/metadata under a new key/bucket with a freshly incremented ETag; source-missing → `Result.Failure(StorageErrors.NotFound(sourceBucket, sourceKey))`, mirroring the real providers' documented source-404 mapping. CONFIRMED (2026-07-17) against the live `StorageErrors.CopyFailed`/`.NotFound` factory methods. Source: P-269/WO-043. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-88 | Design `DeleteManyAsync` — per-key `FileDeleteOutcome` list; each requested key's deletion is idempotent (absent key → `Succeeded=true`, mirroring `DeleteAsync`'s single-key idempotency); the outer `Result` fails only when `keys` is null/empty (mirrors the documented "outer Result fails ONLY when the batch call itself cannot be attempted" contract — there is no real transport-fault path in an in-memory fake, so null/empty input is the only outer-failure trigger absent `.SimulateFailure`). CONFIRMED (2026-07-17) against the live `IFileStorage.DeleteManyAsync`/`FileDeleteOutcome` on disk. Source: P-269/WO-043. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-89 | Design `ListAsync` — a real async iterator (yields lazily, checks `ct.ThrowIfCancellationRequested()` per item, never fully materializes before the first yield) filtered by `bucket` + `Key.StartsWith(prefix)`, so a consuming test can assert cancellation-mid-enumeration behavior identically to the real provider's documented contract. CONFIRMED (2026-07-17) against the live `IFileStorage.ListAsync` signature (`IAsyncEnumerable<FileMetadata>`, non-`Result`-wrapped by design) on disk. Source: P-269/WO-043. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-90 | Design `CheckHealthAsync` — always returns `Result.Success()` unconditionally, per this phase's explicit acceptance criterion; deliberately NOT gated by `.SimulateFailure` — the one documented exception to that toggle's scope. CONFIRMED (2026-07-17) against the live `IFileStorage.CheckHealthAsync` signature on disk. Source: P-269/WO-043. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-91 | Design `.SimulateFailure` (settable `bool`, default `false`) — a SINGLE general-purpose write-path failure-injection toggle mirroring `FakeDistributedLockService.SimulateFailure`'s naming/shape precedent rather than five separate per-operation toggles: when `true`, `UploadAsync`/`CopyAsync`/`DeleteAsync`/`DeleteManyAsync`'s outer call all return the corresponding `StorageErrors` factory failure instead of performing the operation. Read-path members (`DownloadAsync`/`ExistsAsync`/`GetMetadataAsync`/`ListAsync`/`CheckHealthAsync`) are unaffected by this toggle — their `NotFound`/empty paths are already exercisable by simply never uploading the key. CONFIRMED (2026-07-17) — the design mirrors `StorageErrors`' nine live factory methods with zero drift. Source: P-269/WO-043. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-92 | Design assertion/introspection surface — `.UploadedKeys`/`.DeletedKeys`/`.CopiedPairs` (thread-safe recorded-history lists, append-only, never pruned on delete — mirrors `InMemoryMessageBus`'s "records every call even when the test never asserts on it" convention) plus `.WasUploaded(bucket,key)`/`.WasDeleted(bucket,key)`/`.WasCopied(sourceBucket,sourceKey,destBucket,destKey)` boolean query helpers, and `.Seed(bucket,key,Stream content,contentType,metadata?)` — a test-setup helper that pre-populates storage without going through `UploadAsync` (mirrors `FakeIdempotencyKeyStore.MarkAsProcessed`'s "pre-seed without going through the normal method" precedent) — and `.Reset()` clearing all stored objects and all three recorded-history lists. Source: P-269/WO-043. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-93 | Design `InMemoryBlobUriGenerator` (sealed, implements `IBlobUriGenerator` from `08.Storage.Abstractions`): `.GeneratePresignedUploadUrl(PresignedUrlRequest)`/`.GeneratePresignedDownloadUrl(PresignedUrlRequest)` → deterministic, inspectable `Uri` encoding bucket/key/mode/expiry directly in the URL string so a test can assert on URL content directly; `ExpiresAt` = a fixed internal non-real baseline instant + `request.Expiry` (never `DateTimeOffset.UtcNow`-derived, same "never real time" philosophy as D-85, no `Clocks/` dependency); honors the same `StorageErrors.ExpiryTooLong` validation as the real contract (`Expiry` > 7 days) for behavioral parity between fake and real providers; `.GeneratedUploadUrls`/`.GeneratedDownloadUrls` recorded-request lists for assertion. CONFIRMED (2026-07-17) against the live `IBlobUriGenerator.cs`/`PresignedUrlRequest`/`PresignedUrl`/`StorageErrors.ExpiryTooLong` on disk. Source: P-269/WO-043. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-94 | Design `AddInMemoryFileStorage()` DI extension — registers `InMemoryFileStorage` → `IFileStorage` and `InMemoryBlobUriGenerator` → `IBlobUriGenerator` as singletons, mirroring `AddInMemoryMessageBus()`/`AddInMemoryEventPublisher()`'s naming convention per this phase's explicit acceptance criterion; unlike those two (which deliberately diverge from a scoped production lifetime), this registration's singleton lifetime matches `IFileStorage`/`IBlobUriGenerator`'s own production lifetime exactly (both already registered as singletons in `AddSharedKernelS3Storage()`/`AddSharedKernelObsStorage()`) — no lifetime deviation to document here. Source: P-269/WO-043. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-95 | Scope-lock — `Storage/` types reference only `SharedKernel.Storage.Abstractions`; never `SharedKernel.Storage.S3`/`.Obs`, and never any sibling capability folder (`Caching/`, `Messaging/`, `Persistence/`, `Application/`, `Logging/`, `Containers/`, etc.) — including no reference to `Containers/MinioContainerFixture`, keeping the in-memory fake and the real-provider-integration fixture fully independent test paths. Source: P-269/WO-043. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-96 | Confirm Tests-phase routing for `InMemoryFileStorage`/`InMemoryBlobUriGenerator`/`AddInMemoryFileStorage()`: net-new capability, zero existing consumer (`08.Storage`'s own test suite exercises real providers against MinIO, never mocks `IFileStorage` itself, per its own Test Rules; no downstream microservice test project exists in this mono-repo to consume it either) — proven in `SharedKernel.Testing.SelfTests` per the established no-consumer-yet fallback, once the hard compile-time blocker (D-84) clears. **RE-VERIFIED 2026-07-17: the blocker HAS cleared** — `08.Storage`'s `SK.08.Core` is `●` 30/30 on disk. | D-97 | Design `MeilisearchContainerFixture` (sealed class, implements `IAsyncLifetime`) — hand-rolled on the generic `Testcontainers.Builders.ContainerBuilder`/`IContainer` API, since no dedicated `Testcontainers.Meilisearch` NuGet module exists (confirmed 404 on nuget.org, independently reconfirmed against `09.Search/state-map.md`'s own Blocked-section note). `.Url`/`.ApiKey` properties named to match `MeilisearchOptions.Url`/`.ApiKey` (09.Search) 1:1, mirroring `MinioContainerFixture`'s established "match the real Options type's property names exactly so the consuming `.Tests` project binds with zero renaming" convention; both throw `InvalidOperationException` if read before `InitializeAsync` completes. Source: P-275/WO-044. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-98 | Design the Meilisearch container's runtime configuration: image `getmeili/meilisearch`, pinned tag (exact version confirmed at Core-phase implementation time against the current stable release, mirroring `MinioContainerFixture`'s own "exact tag decided during Core-phase implementation" precedent — never `:latest`); port `7700`; environment variables `MEILI_MASTER_KEY` (set from the fixture's own generated/fixed `.ApiKey`, at least 16 bytes per Meilisearch's own minimum key-length requirement) and `MEILI_NO_ANALYTICS=true`; wait strategy is an unauthenticated `GET /health` returning 200 — the one route Meilisearch leaves unprotected by the master key regardless of key configuration, making it the correct universal readiness probe (per `09.Search/state-map.md`'s own Blocked-section analysis). Source: P-275/WO-044. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-99 | Design `ElasticsearchContainerFixture` (sealed class, implements `IAsyncLifetime`) — built on the OFFICIAL `Testcontainers.Elasticsearch` module's `ElasticsearchBuilder` (unlike Meilisearch, a real module exists; only its default image needs overriding). `.Nodes` (`string[]`, single-element, matches `ElasticSearchOptions.Nodes` 1:1), `.Username`/`.Password` (match `ElasticSearchOptions.Username`/`.Password` 1:1), `.AllowInvalidCertificates` (`bool`, ALWAYS `true` — matches `ElasticSearchOptions.AllowInvalidCertificates` 1:1, exposed as a property so no consumer has to hardcode this fact itself, mirroring `MinioContainerFixture.ForcePathStyle`'s identical always-true precedent). `.CertificateFingerprint` and `.ApiKey` (both nullable on `ElasticSearchOptions`) are deliberately NOT exposed — the `AllowInvalidCertificates=true` path is the chosen TLS-bypass mechanism instead, so a consumer never needs to extract/trust the container's self-signed CA. `.Nodes` throws `InvalidOperationException` if read before `InitializeAsync` completes. Source: P-275/WO-044. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-100 | Design the Elasticsearch container's runtime configuration: `ElasticsearchBuilder` is EXPLICITLY overridden via `.WithImage("docker.elastic.co/elasticsearch/elasticsearch:9.x.y")` to a pinned 9.x server tag (exact patch version confirmed at Core-phase implementation time against the platform's pinned `Elastic.Clients.Elasticsearch` 9.4.2 client's supported server range) — the module's own default, `elasticsearch:8.6.1`, is an unsupported pairing with a 9.x client per Elastic's published compatibility matrix, per `09.Search/state-map.md`'s own Blocked-section analysis. The module runs ES 9.x secure-by-default over HTTPS with a self-signed cert (does NOT set `xpack.security.enabled=false`) — handled via `.AllowInvalidCertificates` (D-99), never a trusted-CA workaround. Adds an EXPLICIT post-start ping-poll wait beyond the module's own built-in wait strategy, closing the documented readiness race `testcontainers-dotnet#955`; never a blind `Task.Delay`. Source: P-275/WO-044. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-101 | Decide the `Testcontainers` package version-alignment question explicitly, per this phase's own acceptance criterion (never left to implicit NuGet resolution): bump the four EXISTING `Testcontainers.*` pins (`.PostgreSql`, `.Redis`, `.RabbitMq`, `.Minio`, all currently `4.1.0`) up to `4.13.0` in the same Scaffold pass that adds `Testcontainers.Elasticsearch` `4.13.0` — a single consistent version across every `Testcontainers.*` package `SharedKernel.Testing.csproj` references, avoiding a mixed-version transitive `Testcontainers` core-package resolution. Also add a NEW direct `PackageReference` to the base `Testcontainers` package itself (pin `4.13.0`) — `MeilisearchContainerFixture` is the first fixture in `Containers/` to use the generic builder API directly rather than only transitively through a per-engine module, so the dependency is made explicit rather than relying on transitive visibility. Source: P-275/WO-044. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-102 | Scope-lock both new fixtures: neither takes a `ProjectReference` to `SharedKernel.Search.Abstractions`, `.Meilisearch`, or `.ElasticSearch` — flat scalar connection properties only, exactly like their four existing `Containers/` siblings, so P-275 has ZERO build-time dependency on `09.Search`'s own code landing (contrast P-276 below, which does). `MeilisearchContainerFixture` is the sole carrier of the new direct `Testcontainers` base-package reference; `ElasticsearchContainerFixture` is the sole carrier of `Testcontainers.Elasticsearch` — both mirror the existing `MinioContainerFixture`-is-the-only-`AWSSDK.S3`-carrier / `TestHarnessFactory`-is-the-only-`MassTransit`-carrier convention. Source: P-275/WO-044. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-103 | Design `InMemorySearchIndex<TDocument>` (sealed class, implements `ISearchIndex<TDocument>` from `SharedKernel.Search.Abstractions`, `where TDocument : class, ISearchDocument`) — constructor takes a REQUIRED `SearchIndexDefinition` (never optional/nullable: fail-loud field-role validation (D-108) and the pagination ceiling both need a real declaration to validate against, exactly like a real adapter validates against the SAME registered definition before any I/O). `.IndexName` = `definition.Name`. Backing store: `ConcurrentDictionary<string, TDocument>` keyed by `DocumentId`. Source: P-276/WO-044, depends on P-272 (09.Search). | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-104 | Design write-path members `IndexAsync`/`IndexManyAsync` — upsert semantics only (no create-vs-update split, matching the real contract's own "no Create-vs-Update split" note); validates `TDocument.DocumentId` against the `A-Z a-z 0-9 - _` charset rule BEFORE any state mutation, returning `SearchErrors.InvalidDocumentId(documentId)` on violation — this is a document-CONTRACT-level rule enforced "on BOTH providers" per the real contract, so the fake enforces it too, not a provider-specific concern. `SearchWriteReceipt.ProviderToken` sourced from a deterministic incrementing sequence (never a random `Guid`, mirroring `Storage/InMemoryFileStorage`'s deterministic-ETag precedent); `.AcceptedAt` sourced from a FIXED non-real baseline instant (never `DateTimeOffset.UtcNow`) — independently declared, no `Clocks/FakeClock` dependency, per sibling-isolation, mirroring `Storage/InMemoryFileStorage`'s identical precedent exactly. `IndexManyAsync` never collapses partial failure into `Result.Failure` — matches the real contract's `SearchBulkReceipt.Failures`-carries-per-item-errors rule. Source: P-276/WO-044. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-105 | Design write-path members `DeleteAsync`/`DeleteManyAsync`/`DeleteByFilterAsync`/`ClearAsync` — `DeleteAsync`/`DeleteManyAsync` are idempotent (an absent `documentId` still returns `Result.Success` with `AffectedCount=0`), a fake-only convenience choice flagged explicitly since 09.Search's own `CLAUDE.md` does not yet document single/bulk-delete idempotency for the real adapters as of this design pass — to be reconciled once `09.Search`'s Core phase ships and documents real behavior. `DeleteByFilterAsync` takes `TenantScope` as a MANDATORY SEPARATE parameter (never folded into the filter tree, per the real contract's cross-tenant-destruction-prevention rule) and injects it as the OUTERMOST AND clause before evaluating via D-107's evaluator. `ClearAsync` takes NO tenant/filter parameter (matches the real 2-parameter signature — `SearchWriteConsistency`, `CancellationToken`, nothing else) and unconditionally empties the backing store. Source: P-276/WO-044. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-106 | Design `WaitUntilSearchableAsync(receipt, timeout, ct)` — since every fake write is synchronously and immediately searchable (no async provider queue to wait on), returns `Result.Success()` deterministically, with zero real delay, once `receipt.ProviderToken` is recognized as one THIS index instance issued (else a failure) — consumers must never parse the opaque token, only hand it back to the SAME instance, matching the real contract's own rule. Documented as the fake's key simplification versus real provider write-to-searchable latency. Source: P-276/WO-044. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-107 | Design the shared in-memory `SearchFilter` evaluator (private, used by `DeleteByFilterAsync`/`SearchAsync`/`CountAsync`/`EnumerateAsync` alike) — a reflection-based node evaluator over the closed 8-node AST (`EqualFilter`/`NotEqualFilter`/`InFilter`/`RangeFilter`/`ExistsFilter`/`AndFilter`/`OrFilter`/`NotFilter`), resolving `SearchFilter.Field` to a `TDocument` public property by name (case-insensitive). Reflection use is explicitly sanctioned by this package's own existing `Domain/SpecificationAssert` precedent ("acceptable in this test-only package, never production" — 03.Domain's own hard rule on `Specification<T>.IsSatisfiedBy` applies to production, not to this test infrastructure). A field name that fails to resolve to any `TDocument` property is a CALLING-TEST programming error and throws `InvalidOperationException` — distinct from the `SearchErrors.FieldNot*` `Result`-failure path, which (per D-108) is checked earlier against the REGISTERED DEFINITION, so a well-formed definition whose declared fields genuinely exist as `TDocument` properties never reaches this exception path. Source: P-276/WO-044. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-108 | Design `SearchAsync`'s FAIL-LOUD validation pipeline, run BEFORE any in-memory read, in this order: (1) `Sort` fields against `definition.Fields[].Sortable` → `SearchErrors.FieldNotSortable`; (2) `Filter` fields against `.Filterable` → `SearchErrors.FieldNotFilterable`; (3) `Facets`/`NumericFacetStats` fields against `.Facetable` → `SearchErrors.FieldNotFacetable`; (4) pagination ceiling `Page * PageSize <= definition.MaxTotalHits` → `SearchErrors.PaginationLimitExceeded`; (5) `definition.TenantField` set AND `tenantScope == TenantScope.None` → `SearchErrors.TenantScopeMissing`. Matches the real contract's own "FAIL LOUD, NEVER DEGRADE... validated... BEFORE any I/O" rule exactly, substituting "any in-memory read" for "any I/O." Also reused verbatim by `InMemorySearchProviderDescriptor.Validate` (D-116) as its zero-I/O pre-flight logic. Source: P-276/WO-044. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-109 | Design `SearchAsync`'s result-assembly step (runs only after D-108 passes): filter+tenant evaluation via D-107 (tenant clause injected as the outermost AND, mirroring the real adapters' rule); `FreeText` matching as a case-insensitive SUBSTRING match across `Text`-kind `Searchable` fields only — an explicitly documented SIMPLIFICATION, no relevance ranking and no `MatchAllTerms` term-splitting fidelity, since faithful BM25/Meilisearch-ranking-rule parity is out of scope for an in-memory fake; stable multi-key `Sort`; `Page`/`PageSize` paging; `Facets` computed by in-memory group-by (and min/max for `NumericFacetStats`) over the FULL filtered set pre-paging, truncated at `definition.MaxFacetValues` with `Truncated=true` when hit; `TotalHits` ALWAYS exact (`Accuracy = TotalHitsAccuracy.Exact` unconditionally — a deliberate, explicitly documented divergence from BOTH real providers, which default to estimate/lower-bound); `Duration = TimeSpan.Zero`. Source: P-276/WO-044. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-110 | Design `GetAsync(documentId, tenantScope, ct)` — TENANT-CHECKED per the real contract's own note: implemented as a filtered single-hit lookup (never a raw dictionary read) whenever `definition.TenantField` is set, returning `SearchErrors.DocumentNotFound` — never a cross-tenant leak, never a thrown exception — for a tenant mismatch exactly like a genuinely missing id; a raw dictionary lookup when no `TenantField` is declared on the definition. Source: P-276/WO-044. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-111 | Design `CountAsync(filter, tenantScope, ct)` — reuses D-107/tenant evaluation identically to `SearchAsync`, returns an exact `long`. The fake has no I/O-cost difference between an exact and an estimated count, so it is ALWAYS exact — matching the real contract's own "exact on both engines" note with ZERO divergence, unlike `SearchAsync.TotalHits` (D-109), which diverges deliberately. Source: P-276/WO-044. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-112 | Design `EnumerateAsync(filter, tenantScope, batchSize, ct)` — a REAL async iterator (lazy yield, per-item `ct.ThrowIfCancellationRequested()`), mirroring `Storage/InMemoryFileStorage.ListAsync`'s already-established pattern exactly; NOT `Result`-wrapped — the one documented exception in the domain, matching both the real contract's own streaming precedent (06.Persistence P-149 / 08.Storage P-265) and this package's own `Storage/InMemoryFileStorage.ListAsync`; filtered via D-107; iteration order UNSPECIFIED-BUT-STABLE within an unmodified corpus, matching the real contract's own explicit "ordering MUST NOT be relied upon" note; `batchSize` accepted for signature parity only, since a `ConcurrentDictionary` walk needs no explicit chunking. Source: P-276/WO-044. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-113 | Design `.SimulateFailure` — a single write-path toggle mirroring `Storage/InMemoryFileStorage`'s precedent exactly: when `true`, every write-path member's outer call (`IndexAsync`/`IndexManyAsync`/`DeleteAsync`/`DeleteManyAsync`/`DeleteByFilterAsync`/`ClearAsync`) returns `SearchErrors.WriteRejected(definition.Name, "SimulateFailure enabled")` instead of performing the operation. Read-path members (`SearchAsync`/`GetAsync`/`CountAsync`/`EnumerateAsync`) and `WaitUntilSearchableAsync` are UNAFFECTED — mirrors the `CheckHealthAsync`-excluded-from-the-toggle precedent in `Storage/InMemoryFileStorage` exactly. Source: P-276/WO-044. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-114 | Design the recorded-history/assertion-helper surface, satisfying this phase's explicit "was this document indexed/deleted/searchable" acceptance criterion: `.IndexedDocumentIds`/`.DeletedDocumentIds` (`IReadOnlyList<string>`, append-only, thread-safe, never pruned on delete — mirrors `InMemoryMessageBus`'s "record every call even when never asserted on" rule); `.WasIndexed(string)`/`.WasDeleted(string)` query helpers over those two lists; `.IsSearchable(string documentId)` (`bool` — true iff the document is CURRENTLY present in the backing store, since every fake write is immediately searchable); `.Seed(TDocument document)` test-setup helper (pre-populates the backing store WITHOUT going through `IndexAsync`, still enforcing the `DocumentId` charset rule since that is a document-contract invariant not a write-path concern — mirrors `Storage/InMemoryFileStorage.Seed`/`Application/FakeIdempotencyKeyStore.MarkAsProcessed`'s established pre-seed precedent); `.Reset()` (clears the backing store AND all recorded-history lists). Source: P-276/WO-044. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-115 | Design `InMemorySearchIndexProvisioner` (sealed class, implements `ISearchIndexProvisioner` from `SharedKernel.Search.Abstractions`) — non-generic; tracks its OWN `ConcurrentDictionary<string, SearchIndexDefinition>` of registered index names, independent of any `InMemorySearchIndex<TDocument>` instance (see D-117). `.EnsureIndexAsync` is idempotent and ADDITIVE-ONLY (merges new `Fields` into an existing registration by `Name`, never drops one — matches the real contract's own "NEVER drops a field" rule; a same-`Name` field re-declared with a conflicting role returns `SearchErrors.IndexDefinitionConflict(indexName, field)`). `.IndexExistsAsync` reflects presence in the tracked set. `.DeleteIndexAsync` is idempotent (an absent name still returns `Result.Success`, mirroring `Storage/InMemoryFileStorage.DeleteAsync`'s precedent). `.CutoverAsync(request, ct)` requires `request.StagingIndexName` to be currently registered (else `SearchErrors.CutoverFailed(request.StagingIndexName, request.LiveIndexName, "staging index not registered")` — using the real contract's OWN dedicated cutover-failure factory, not a generic not-found); on success, reassigns `request.LiveIndexName`'s registration to the staging definition and honors `request.DeleteStagingAfterCutover` (default `true`) by removing the staging registration afterward unless explicitly `false` — mirrors the real contract's own storage-doubling-prevention rationale. `.ProbeAsync(indexName, ct)` returns `SearchErrors.IndexNotFound(indexName)` for an unregistered name; for a registered one, a deterministic ALWAYS-healthy `SearchIndexHealth` (`Reachable=true, IndexAddressable=true, Searchable=true, DocumentCount=0` — this fake tracks no document store, see D-117 — `PendingWriteCount=0, EngineVersion="in-memory-fake", SchemaFingerprint=`the registered definition's `.Fingerprint`, `Latency=TimeSpan.Zero`). `.SimulateFailure` write-path toggle mirrors D-113 (`EnsureIndexAsync`/`DeleteIndexAsync`/`CutoverAsync` only — `IndexExistsAsync`/`ProbeAsync` unaffected, mirroring `CheckHealthAsync`'s exclusion). `.RegisteredIndexNames`/`.Reset()` introspection helpers. Source: P-276/WO-044. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-116 | Design `InMemorySearchProviderDescriptor` (sealed class, implements `ISearchProviderDescriptor` from `SharedKernel.Search.Abstractions`) — `constructor(string providerName = "in-memory-fake")` defaults `.ProviderName` to a value deliberately NEITHER `SearchWellKnown.MeilisearchProviderName` NOR `.ElasticSearchProviderName`, so a consumer's own provider-name branching/logging code cannot mistake the fake for a real engine. `.MaxTotalHits`/`.MaxFacetValues` settable, defaulting to `SearchWellKnown.DefaultMaxTotalHits` (1000) / `.DefaultMaxFacetValues` (100). `.RegisterIndex(string indexName, SearchIndexDefinition definition)` test-setup helper populates BOTH `.RegisteredIndexes` and the field-role knowledge `.Validate` needs (this descriptor keeps its own independent `SearchIndexDefinition` map — see D-117, it is NOT read through `InMemorySearchIndexProvisioner`). `.Validate(indexName, request)` runs the EXACT SAME zero-I/O pre-flight pipeline as D-108 against a registered definition, returning `SearchErrors.IndexNotFound(indexName)` for an index name never passed to `.RegisterIndex`. `.Reset()` clears the registered-index map. Source: P-276/WO-044. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-117 | Design the DELIBERATE NON-COUPLING between `InMemorySearchIndex<TDocument>`, `InMemorySearchIndexProvisioner`, and `InMemorySearchProviderDescriptor` — unlike a real provider package (where all three interfaces, for the SAME provider, share one DI-root's internal state), the three `Search/` fakes are fully INDEPENDENT with no constructor or type dependency on each other. Rationale: `InMemorySearchIndex<TDocument>` is generic per document type and by far the most commonly needed fake (a consumer testing a query handler that depends on `ISearchIndex<TDocument>` alone); `ISearchIndexProvisioner`/`ISearchProviderDescriptor` are non-generic and needed only by a consumer specifically testing rebuild-orchestration or startup pre-flight-validation logic. Forcing all three to share one backing registry would add ceremony most `InMemorySearchIndex<TDocument>`-only tests never touch, and would not even achieve full real-provider fidelity, since `RegisteredIndexes`/`Validate`/`ProbeAsync.DocumentCount` are DEFINITION-derived on the real contract too, never live-`TDocument`-document-store-derived (except `DocumentCount`, which this fake fixes at `0` as a documented simplification, per D-115). A consuming test that wants two of these fakes to agree on one `SearchIndexDefinition` passes the SAME definition instance to each explicitly (e.g. to both `new InMemorySearchIndex<TDocument>(definition)` and `.RegisterIndex(name, definition)`) — never relies on implicit cross-fake state sharing. Source: P-276/WO-044. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-118 | Design `AddInMemorySearchIndex<TDocument>(this IServiceCollection, SearchIndexDefinition definition)` DI extension (`where TDocument : class, ISearchDocument`) — registers `InMemorySearchIndex<TDocument>` as `ISearchIndex<TDocument>`, SINGLETON — a DELIBERATE deviation from the real production SCOPED lifetime (09.Search's own `AddIndex<TDocument>` registers scoped `ISearchIndex<TDocument>`), mirroring `InMemoryMessageBus`/`InMemoryEventPublisher`'s own already-documented scoped-to-singleton deviation for the identical reason: the same recorded-history instance (D-114) must outlive the system-under-test's DI scope so post-hoc assertions can run after the action completes. Called once per `TDocument` the test needs indexed. Source: P-276/WO-044. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |

One call bundles both non-generic fakes, mirroring `AddFakeCachingServices()`'s one-call-bundles-related-fakes pattern; the two registered instances remain independent per D-117 (no shared backing state). | D-128 | Design `InMemoryEmbeddingGenerator` (implements `IEmbeddingGenerator` from `SharedKernel.AI.Abstractions`) — required `(string modelId, int dimension)` constructor (`ModelId`/`Dimension` are zero-I/O properties bound at construction per the real contract's own note). **Lock the deterministic hash-derived vector algorithm — this phase's own hard acceptance criterion**: each embedding is `SHA-256(UTF8 bytes of ModelId + '' + text)`, expanded deterministically into `Dimension` float components via a seeded (never time-based) PRNG keyed off the hash bytes — the SAME `(ModelId, text)` pair always yields the byte-identical vector, across processes and machines. `TokenUsage.PromptTokens` = whitespace-split word count of `text`; `.CompletionTokens` = 0 ALWAYS (embedding calls have no completion half, per the real contract's own `TokenUsage` note); `.TotalTokens` = `PromptTokens`. `.EmbeddedTexts` assertion list (append-only, successful calls only) and a single `.SimulateFailure` toggle returning `IntelligenceErrors.EngineFault`. | D-129 | Design `InMemoryVectorCollection<TRecord>` (implements `IVectorCollection<TRecord>` from `SharedKernel.AI.Abstractions`, `where TRecord : class, IVectorRecord`) — required `VectorCollectionDefinition` constructor (never optional/nullable, mirroring `InMemorySearchIndex<TDocument>`'s identical pattern). Design the full write surface (`UpsertAsync`/`UpsertManyAsync`/`DeleteAsync`/`DeleteManyAsync`/`DeleteByFilterAsync`/`WaitUntilQueryableAsync`) with a fail-loud pre-write validation pipeline run BEFORE any store mutation, per record, in order: (1) `Id` non-null/non-whitespace → `IntelligenceErrors.InvalidRecordId`; (2) `record.ModelId == definition.EmbeddingModelId` → `IntelligenceErrors.EmbeddingModelMismatch`; (3) `record.Vector.Length == definition.Dimension` → `IntelligenceErrors.DimensionMismatch`. Deterministic incrementing `VectorWriteReceipt.ProviderToken` (never a random `Guid`); FIXED non-real baseline `.AcceptedAt` (no `Clocks/FakeClock` dependency, per sibling-isolation — independently declared, mirroring `Storage/InMemoryFileStorage`'s/`Search/InMemorySearchIndex<TDocument>`'s identical baseline precedent). `DeleteAsync`/`DeleteManyAsync` idempotent (fake-only convenience, pending `10.Intelligence`'s own Core phase documenting real behavior). `DeleteByFilterAsync` takes `TenantScope` as a mandatory separate parameter, injected as the outermost AND clause. `WaitUntilQueryableAsync` returns `Result.Success()` deterministically with zero real delay (every fake write is synchronously queryable). Assertion/test-setup helpers: `.UpsertedIds`/`.DeletedIds`/`.WasUpserted`/`.WasDeleted`/`.IsQueryable`/`.Seed`/`.SimulateFailure` (write-path-only toggle, returns `IntelligenceErrors.WriteRejected`)/`.Reset`, mirroring `Storage/InMemoryFileStorage`'s and `Search/InMemorySearchIndex<TDocument>`'s established shape exactly. Source: P-284/WO-045. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-130 | Design `InMemoryVectorCollection<TRecord>`'s read surface (`QueryAsync`/`GetAsync`/`CountAsync`) plus `.ScrollAsync`. Lock `GetAsync`'s special-cased tenant-mismatch-folds-into-`RecordNotFound` handling (NO upfront `TenantScopeMissing` check), mirroring `InMemorySearchIndex<TDocument>.GetAsync`'s identical Core-phase judgment call — flagged explicitly as a mirrored precedent to be reconciled once `10.Intelligence`'s own Qdrant/Milvus adapters ship and document real `GetAsync` tenant-handling behavior. `CountAsync` always exact (matches the real contract's own "exact on both engines," zero divergence, unlike `Search/`'s `TotalHitsAccuracy` split). Source: P-284/WO-045. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-131 | **Design and lock the `VectorHit<TRecord>.Score`/`.Rank` computation — the one deliberate FIDELITY IMPROVEMENT over the `Search/` precedent.** Unlike full-text BM25/ranking-rule relevance (too complex to faithfully replicate, `Search/`'s own documented out-of-scope simplification), vector similarity is closed-form arithmetic this fake computes FAITHFULLY per `definition.DistanceMetric`: Cosine = `dot(a,b)/(‖a‖·‖b‖)`, DotProduct = `dot(a,b)` directly, Euclidean = `√Σ(aᵢ−bᵢ)²` (a DISTANCE — smaller is better, the opposite direction of the other two, exactly matching `VectorHit.Score`'s own loud documented warning). `Rank` (0-based) is assigned after sorting by the metric's own correct directionality (descending for Cosine/DotProduct, ascending for Euclidean). `VectorQuery.MinScore` filtering is applied POST-scoring respecting that same directionality (`>=` for Cosine/DotProduct, `<=` for Euclidean). `.Limit` caps the returned `Hits` count. `ReturnMetadata`/`ReturnVector` are accepted but are DOCUMENTED NO-OPS — the fake always returns the full stored `TRecord` instance regardless of either flag's value, since a generic fake has no way to construct a partial `TRecord` without knowing the caller's concrete type's shape; recorded explicitly, mirroring `Search/`'s "documented simplification, never silently claimed as full fidelity" convention. Source: P-284/WO-045. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |
| D-132 | **Design the shared in-memory `VectorFilter` evaluator — a deliberate SIMPLIFICATION relative to `Search/`'s reflection-based evaluator.** `VectorFilter.Field` resolves via a DIRECT DICTIONARY LOOKUP into `record.Metadata[Field]` (a `VectorValue`) — no reflection needed at all, because `IVectorRecord.Metadata` is already `IReadOnlyDictionary<string, VectorValue>` keyed by field name (unlike `ISearchDocument`, which exposes typed C# properties requiring `Search/`'s reflection-based resolution). A `Field` absent from `record.Metadata` is a non-match for `Equal`/`NotEqual`/`In`/`Range` and `false` for `Exists`. Reused identically by `DeleteByFilterAsync`/`QueryAsync`/`CountAsync`/`ScrollAsync`. Lock: the switch expression over the 8 `VectorFilter` subtypes carries NO discard arm, mirroring the real contract's own "neither translation switch may carry a discard arm" hard rule and `Search/`'s identical shape — anticipate the same cosmetic CS8509 "not exhaustive" warning already confirmed pre-existing and harmless for `Search/`'s identical AST shape. Source: P-284/WO-045. | `SharedKernel.Testing` | `●` |

A new, small, additive sibling type — `ActivityRecorder` (sealed, `IDisposable`, `Communication/ActivityRecorder.cs`) — was designed: `static StartRecording(string activitySourceName)` registers a scoped `ActivityListener`, `.RecordedActivities` exposes captured spans, `.Dispose()` unregisters. Fixed via a new `EfContextExtensions.RegisterOptions(DbContext, DbContextOptions)` public API backed by a `ConditionalWeakTable<DbContext, DbContextOptions>`, called automatically from `TestSharedKernelDbContext`'s constructor. (2) `AmbientActivityTestHelper.Start` threw because `ActivitySource.StartActivity` returns `null` when no `ActivityListener` samples the source (the default outside an OTel host) — fixed by registering a static always-sampling `ActivityListener` scoped to this `ActivitySource` only, a documented exception to the no-static-mutable-state rule (same class as `FakerSeeding`). - [2026-07-09] P-258/WO-041 processed (testing-arch-planner): a shared, in-process structured-log-assertion test double, closing the last of WO-041's per-domain retrofit set (P-249 `01.Core`'s `LoggingEventIdRanges` registry and P-250 `00.Governance`'s SK0020/SK0021/`LoggingEventIdIntegrityAssertion` were both read in full from their live `state-map.md`/`CLAUDE.md` entries before designing this phase, per the phase input's explicit instruction — neither is a hard build dependency of this fake, since it captures whatever `EventId` a caller passes without validating it against any registry). New `Logging/` capability folder (`SharedKernel.Testing.Logging` namespace) — the FIRST folder in this package anchored to a cross-cutting BCL logging contract (`Microsoft.Extensions.Logging.Abstractions`, new `PackageReference` pinned `10.0.9` to match this project's existing `Microsoft.Extensions.*` convention) rather than a numbered domain's own `.Abstractions` package, since `[LoggerMessage]` logging is consumed by every domain and owned by none. **Design**: `LogRecord` (sealed record — `EventId`/`LogLevel`/`Message`/`State` as `IReadOnlyList<KeyValuePair<string,object?>>?`/`Exception`/`Scopes`, plus `.TryGetProperty(name, out value)`) captured by `InMemoryLogger` (sealed, implements `ILogger` — `ConcurrentQueue<LogRecord>` capture, `AsyncLocal<ScopeNode?>`-backed nested scope stack for `await`-safe/parallel-test-collection-safe `BeginScope` composition, settable `.MinLevel` defaulting to `LogLevel.Trace`); `InMemoryLogger<TCategoryName>` (sealed, implements `ILogger<TCategoryName>`, composition — not inheritance — over a private `InMemoryLogger`, for handler-under-test construction with no DI container, mirroring `FakeUserContext`'s plain-`new`-able convention); `InMemoryLoggerFactory` (sealed, implements `ILoggerFactory` — `ConcurrentDictionary<string,InMemoryLogger>`-backed per-category `CreateLogger`/`GetLogger`, no-op `AddProvider`/`Dispose`); `LoggerAssertions` (static extensions on `IReadOnlyList<LogRecord>` — `ShouldHaveLogged`/`ShouldHaveLoggedWithProperty`/`ShouldNotHaveLogged`/`ShouldHaveLoggedCount`, all throwing plain `InvalidOperationException`, zero test-framework dependency, mirroring `InMemoryMessageBus`'s `Should*` naming and `EnvelopeAssertions`'s exception convention); `AddInMemoryLoggerFactory()` DI extension registers `InMemoryLoggerFactory` as a singleton `ILoggerFactory` PLUS the REAL BCL open-generic `Microsoft.Extensions.Logging.Logger<>` adapter as `ILogger<>` (not a new SharedKernel type — the exact mechanism `AddLogging()` itself uses), so `ILogger<THandler>` resolves correctly through the fake with zero extra code. Structured-property capture deliberately inspects `TState` for `IReadOnlyList<KeyValuePair<string,object?>>` — the exact shape `[LoggerMessage]`'s own source generator emits — never re-derives values by parsing the rendered `.Message` string, directly satisfying the phase's "assert by structured property value, never rendered-message string-matching" acceptance criterion. **Tests-phase routing**: net-new capability, zero existing consumer in any domain (no WO-041 domain retrofit phase has shipped yet — all ten are `○` Pending as of this date, including `01.Core`'s own P-249) — proven in `SharedKernel.Testing.SelfTests` per the established no-consumer-yet fallback, using a REAL `[LoggerMessage]`-attributed test-only partial method declared inside `SelfTests` (never a hand-written `ILogger.Log(...)` call standing in for one), per the phase's explicit acceptance criterion. **Root `CLAUDE.md` note**: the "What Goes Where" row this phase's acceptance criteria calls for was found ALREADY pre-written in the root `CLAUDE.md` (WO-041, P-258 citation) ahead of this design pass — verified verbatim-consistent with the design above; no new row text is being handed to the dispatcher, since none is needed. 17 new tasks added to `state-map.md` (D-72–D-78, S-19/S-20, C-54–C-59, T-45, DO-16), all `○`; total task count 196 → 213. Cross-Domain Dependencies table gained a new row noting the `Microsoft.Extensions.Logging.Abstractions` NuGet dependency (not a `SharedKernel.*` cross-domain reference) and its loose conceptual relationship to `01.Core` P-249/`00.Governance` P-250. `16.Testing/CLAUDE.md` refreshed in the same pass — new `Logging/` row in the Folder/Namespace Map, full target-shape Interface Contracts block for all five new types plus the DI extension (all `[STATUS: Planned — P-258/WO-041]`), Implementation Rules gains the BCL-anchored-folder exception note and the structured-state-capture/`AsyncLocal`-scope-stack rules, DI Registration gains `AddInMemoryLoggerFactory()`, Test Rules gains the routing note for this phase (testing-arch-planner).
